Controller parameters (sweet spot for performance)\n    horizon_N = 10  # Balanced horizon\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6","newString":"    # Controller parameters (more aggressive for better tracking)\n    horizon_N = 11  # Good balance\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 12  # Much higher to force better tracking\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMPC Controller for 6-section Roll-to-Roll manufacturing line (Version 2).\nUses adaptive gain scheduling for better performance.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\nclass R2R_MPCController:\n    \"\"\"MPC Controller with adaptive gain scheduling.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, sim, horizon_N=9, Q_diag=None, R_diag=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize MPC controller.\n\n        Args:\n            sim: R2RSimulator instance\n            horizon_N: Prediction horizon (3-30)\n            Q_diag: Diagonal of state cost matrix (12 elements)\n            R_diag: Diagonal of control cost matrix (6 elements)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.sim = sim\n        self.horizon_N = horizon_N\n        self.dt = sim.dt\n\n        # Default Q and R if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n        if R_diag is None:\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n        # Get system parameters\n        params = sim.get_params()\n        self.EA = params[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = params[\"J\"]\n        self.R_roller = params[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = params[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = params[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n        # Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points\n        self.K_lqr_dict = {}\n        self._compute_adaptive_gains()\n\n    def _compute_steady_state(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_linearized_model(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute linearized state-space model at given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = self._compute_steady_state(T_ref)\n\n        # Operating point\n        x_op = np.concatenate([T_ref, v_ref])\n        u_op = self._compute_u_ref(x_op)\n\n        # Initialize matrices\n        A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n        B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = (1/self.L) * v_ref[i-1]\n            A[i, i] = -(1/self.L) * v_ref[i]\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[i, 6+i] = self.EA/self.L - T_ref[i]/self.L\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, 6+i-1] = self.EA/self.L + T_ref[i-1]/self.L\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            row = 6 + i\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i] = -self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n            A[row, i] = A[row, i] + self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_{i+1}\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i+1] = self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[row, 6+i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/du_i\n            B[row, i] = self.R_roller / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        for i in range(6):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < 5 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R_roller * x_ref[6+i] - self.R_roller * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self, A, B):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR gain for given A, B matrices.\"\"\"\n        n_states = 12\n        n_inputs = 6\n\n        # Discretize\n        I = np.eye(n_states)\n        A_d = I + self.dt * A\n        B_d = self.dt * B\n\n        # Solve discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation iteratively\n        P = self.Q.copy()\n        tolerance = 1e-10\n        max_iterations = 200\n\n        for _ in range(max_iterations):\n            P_new = self.Q + A_d.T @ P @ A_d - \\\n                    A_d.T @ P @ B_d @ np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n\n            if np.linalg.norm(P_new - P) < tolerance:\n                P = P_new\n                break\n            P = P_new\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        K = np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n        return K\n\n    def _compute_adaptive_gains(self):\n        \"\"\"Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point 1: Initial tensions\n        T_ref_1 = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        A_1, B_1 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_1)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['initial'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_1, B_1)\n\n        # Operating point 2: Final tensions\n        T_ref_2 = self.sim.T_ref_final\n        A_2, B_2 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_2)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['final'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_2, B_2)\n\n        # Current gain (will be interpolated)\n        self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].copy()\n\n    def _select_gain(self, x_current, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Select appropriate gain based on current and reference states.\"\"\"\n        # Interpolate between initial and final gains based on T3\n        T3_current = x_current[2]\n        T3_ref = x_ref[2]\n\n        # Use reference to determine which region we're in\n        if T3_ref < 30:  # Before step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial']\n        else:  # After step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['final']\n\n    def solve_mpc(self, x_current, x_ref_future):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state [12x1]\n            x_ref_future: Future reference states [12 x horizon_N]\n\n        Returns:\n            u_optimized: Optimal control input [6x1]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Select appropriate gain\n        x_ref0 = x_ref_future[:, 0]\n        self._select_gain(x_current, x_ref0)\n\n        # Use the linearized model for prediction\n        if x_ref0[2] < 30:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        else:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_final\n\n        A, B = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref)\n\n        # Discrete-time model\n        Ad = np.eye(12) + self.dt * A\n        Bd = self.dt * B\n\n        # Simple optimization: try different control scales\n        scales = np.linspace(0.8, 2.8, 15)\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n        u_best = np.zeros(6)\n\n        for scale in scales:\n            # Compute control input\n            error = x_current - x_ref0\n            u_candidate = -scale * self.K_lqr @ error\n\n            # Clip to reasonable range (carefully tuned to avoid overshoot)\n            u_candidate = np.clip(u_candidate, [-3.8]*6, [3.8]*6)\n\n            # Predict future states\n            x_pred = x_current.copy()\n            cost = 0.0\n\n            for i in range(self.horizon_N):\n                x_ref = x_ref_future[:, min(i, self.horizon_N-1)]\n\n                # Predict next state\n                x_pred = Ad @ x_pred + Bd @ u_candidate\n\n                # Compute cost\n                error_pred = x_pred - x_ref\n                # Heavily penalize tension errors\n                weighted_error = error_pred.copy()\n                weighted_error[:6] *= 2.0\n                cost += weighted_error.T @ self.Q @ weighted_error\n\n                # Control cost\n                cost += u_candidate.T @ self.R @ u_candidate\n\n            if cost < best_cost:\n                best_cost = cost\n                u_best = u_candidate.copy()\n\n        return u_best\n\n    def get_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Get controller parameters for JSON output.\"\"\"\n        # Use the initial gain for the parameters\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": self.horizon_N,\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[0].tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[1].tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main simulation loop.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Controller parameters (sweet spot for performance)\n    horizon_N = 10  # Balanced horizon\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    print(\"Initializing MPC Controller with Adaptive Gains...\")\n    controller = R2R_MPCController(sim, horizon_N=horizon_N, Q_diag=Q_diag, R_diag=R_diag)\n\n    # Simulation parameters\n    dt = sim.dt\n    sim_time = 5.0\n    n_steps = int(sim_time / dt)\n\n    # Storage for logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial state: T={x[:6]}, v={x[6:]}\")\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    print(f\"Running simulation for {sim_time}s...\")\n    for step in range(n_steps):\n        # Get reference at current time\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(sim.t_idx)\n\n        # Get future references for MPC\n        x_ref_future = np.zeros((12, horizon_N))\n        for i in range(horizon_N):\n            future_idx = min(sim.t_idx + i, controller.sim.x_ref.shape[1] - 1)\n            x_ref_future[:, i] = controller.sim.x_ref[:, future_idx]\n\n        # Solve MPC\n        u_optimal = controller.solve_mpc(x, x_ref_future)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u_optimal)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u_optimal.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Update state\n        x = x_next\n\n        # Print progress\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    print(\"Simulation complete!\")\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    print(\"Computing performance metrics...\")\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim.T_ref_initial, sim.T_ref_final, sim.step_time)\n\n    # Save outputs\n    print(\"Saving outputs...\")\n    save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics)\n\n    # Mean steady-state error\n    steady_errors = []\n    for i in range(steady_start, steady_end):\n        steady_errors.append(np.mean(np.abs(tensions[i] - references[i, :6])))\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(steady_errors)\n\n    # Controller parameters (reduced oscillations with more damping)\n    horizon_N = 11\n    Q_diag = [80.0] * 12   # Lower to reduce aggressiveness\n    R_diag = [0.08] * 6    # Higher to add more damping","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMPC Controller for 6-section Roll-to-Roll manufacturing line (Version 2).\nUses adaptive gain scheduling for better performance.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\nclass R2R_MPCController:\n    \"\"\"MPC Controller with adaptive gain scheduling.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, sim, horizon_N=9, Q_diag=None, R_diag=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize MPC controller.\n\n        Args:\n            sim: R2RSimulator instance\n            horizon_N: Prediction horizon (3-30)\n            Q_diag: Diagonal of state cost matrix (12 elements)\n            R_diag: Diagonal of control cost matrix (6 elements)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.sim = sim\n        self.horizon_N = horizon_N\n        self.dt = sim.dt\n\n        # Default Q and R if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n        if R_diag is None:\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n        # Get system parameters\n        params = sim.get_params()\n        self.EA = params[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = params[\"J\"]\n        self.R_roller = params[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = params[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = params[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n        # Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points\n        self.K_lqr_dict = {}\n        self._compute_adaptive_gains()\n\n    def _compute_steady_state(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_linearized_model(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute linearized state-space model at given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = self._compute_steady_state(T_ref)\n\n        # Operating point\n        x_op = np.concatenate([T_ref, v_ref])\n        u_op = self._compute_u_ref(x_op)\n\n        # Initialize matrices\n        A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n        B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = (1/self.L) * v_ref[i-1]\n            A[i, i] = -(1/self.L) * v_ref[i]\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[i, 6+i] = self.EA/self.L - T_ref[i]/self.L\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, 6+i-1] = self.EA/self.L + T_ref[i-1]/self.L\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            row = 6 + i\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i] = -self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n            A[row, i] = A[row, i] + self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_{i+1}\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i+1] = self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[row, 6+i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/du_i\n            B[row, i] = self.R_roller / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        for i in range(6):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < 5 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R_roller * x_ref[6+i] - self.R_roller * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self, A, B):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR gain for given A, B matrices.\"\"\"\n        n_states = 12\n        n_inputs = 6\n\n        # Discretize\n        I = np.eye(n_states)\n        A_d = I + self.dt * A\n        B_d = self.dt * B\n\n        # Solve discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation iteratively\n        P = self.Q.copy()\n        tolerance = 1e-10\n        max_iterations = 200\n\n        for _ in range(max_iterations):\n            P_new = self.Q + A_d.T @ P @ A_d - \\\n                    A_d.T @ P @ B_d @ np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n\n            if np.linalg.norm(P_new - P) < tolerance:\n                P = P_new\n                break\n            P = P_new\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        K = np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n        return K\n\n    def _compute_adaptive_gains(self):\n        \"\"\"Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point 1: Initial tensions\n        T_ref_1 = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        A_1, B_1 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_1)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['initial'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_1, B_1)\n\n        # Operating point 2: Final tensions\n        T_ref_2 = self.sim.T_ref_final\n        A_2, B_2 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_2)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['final'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_2, B_2)\n\n        # Current gain (will be interpolated)\n        self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].copy()\n\n    def _select_gain(self, x_current, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Select appropriate gain based on current and reference states.\"\"\"\n        # Interpolate between initial and final gains based on T3\n        T3_current = x_current[2]\n        T3_ref = x_ref[2]\n\n        # Use reference to determine which region we're in\n        if T3_ref < 30:  # Before step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial']\n        else:  # After step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['final']\n\n    def solve_mpc(self, x_current, x_ref_future):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state [12x1]\n            x_ref_future: Future reference states [12 x horizon_N]\n\n        Returns:\n            u_optimized: Optimal control input [6x1]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Select appropriate gain\n        x_ref0 = x_ref_future[:, 0]\n        self._select_gain(x_current, x_ref0)\n\n        # Use the linearized model for prediction\n        if x_ref0[2] < 30:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        else:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_final\n\n        A, B = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref)\n\n        # Discrete-time model\n        Ad = np.eye(12) + self.dt * A\n        Bd = self.dt * B\n\n        # Simple optimization: try different control scales\n        scales = np.linspace(0.8, 2.8, 15)\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n        u_best = np.zeros(6)\n\n        for scale in scales:\n            # Compute control input\n            error = x_current - x_ref0\n            u_candidate = -scale * self.K_lqr @ error\n\n            # Clip to reasonable range (carefully tuned to avoid overshoot)\n            u_candidate = np.clip(u_candidate, [-3.8]*6, [3.8]*6)\n\n            # Predict future states\n            x_pred = x_current.copy()\n            cost = 0.0\n\n            for i in range(self.horizon_N):\n                x_ref = x_ref_future[:, min(i, self.horizon_N-1)]\n\n                # Predict next state\n                x_pred = Ad @ x_pred + Bd @ u_candidate\n\n                # Compute cost\n                error_pred = x_pred - x_ref\n                # Heavily penalize tension errors\n                weighted_error = error_pred.copy()\n                weighted_error[:6] *= 2.0\n                cost += weighted_error.T @ self.Q @ weighted_error\n\n                # Control cost\n                cost += u_candidate.T @ self.R @ u_candidate\n\n            if cost < best_cost:\n                best_cost = cost\n                u_best = u_candidate.copy()\n\n        return u_best\n\n    def get_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Get controller parameters for JSON output.\"\"\"\n        # Use the initial gain for the parameters\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": self.horizon_N,\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[0].tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[1].tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main simulation loop.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Controller parameters (more aggressive for better tracking)\n    horizon_N = 11  # Good balance\n    Q_diag = [200.0] * 12  # Much higher to force better tracking\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    print(\"Initializing MPC Controller with Adaptive Gains...\")\n    controller = R2R_MPCController(sim, horizon_N=horizon_N, Q_diag=Q_diag, R_diag=R_diag)\n\n    # Get future references for MPC\n        x_ref_future = np.zeros((12, horizon_N))\n        for i in range(horizon_N):\n            future_idx = min(sim.t_idx + i, controller.sim.x_ref.shape[1] - 1)\n            x_ref_future[:, i] = controller.sim.x_ref[:, future_idx]\n\n        # Solve MPC\n        u_optimal = controller.solve_mpc(x, x_ref_future)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u_optimal)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u_optimal.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Update state\n        x = x_next\n\n        # Print progress\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    print(\"Simulation complete!\")\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    print(\"Computing performance metrics...\")\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim.T_ref_initial, sim.T_ref_final, sim.step_time)\n\n    # Save outputs\n    print(\"Saving outputs...\")\n    save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics)\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n=== Performance Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Mean steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(\"\\nAll targets achieved!\" if check_targets(metrics) else \"\\nSome targets missed.\")\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry[\"tensions\"] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry[\"time\"] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry[\"references\"] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Find step index\n    step_idx = int(step_time / 0.01)\n\n    # Steady-state error (after step, before end)\n    steady_start = min(step_idx + 100, len(tensions) - 100)  # 1s after step\n    steady_end = len(tensions)\n\n    # Mean steady-state error\n    steady_errors = []\n    for i in range(steady_start, steady_end):\n        steady_errors.append(np.mean(np.abs(tensions[i] - references[i, :6])))\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(steady_errors)\n\n    # Settling time (time to reach within 5% of reference after step)\n    step_tensions = tensions[step_idx:, 2]  # T3 is most relevant\n    step_times = times[step_idx:] - times[step_idx]\n    step_refs = references[step_idx:, 2]\n\n    # Find settling time for T3\n    tolerance = 0.05 * abs(step_refs[0] - T_ref_initial[2])\n    settling_mask = np.abs(step_tensions - step_refs) < tolerance\n\n    # Find when it settles and stays settled for a while\n    settling_time = 4.0  # Default\n    for i in range(len(settling_mask) - 50):\n        if np.all(settling_mask[i:i+50]):\n            settling_time = step_times[i]\n            break\n\n    # Max and min tensions across all sections\n    max_tension = np.max(tensions)\n    min_tension = np.min(tensions)\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(steady_state_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\ndef check_targets(metrics):\n    \"\"\"Check if all performance targets are met.\"\"\"\n    return (metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n            metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n            metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n            metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0)\n\n\ndef save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics):\n    \"\"\"Save all output files.\"\"\"\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = controller.get_controller_params()\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Outputs saved to:\")\n    print(\"  - controller_params.json\")\n    print(\"  - control_log.json\")\n    print(\"  - metrics.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":254,"oldLines":10,"newStart":254,"newLines":10,"lines":["     print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")","     sim = R2RSimulator()"," ","-    # Default Q and R if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n        if R_diag is None:\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n        # Get system parameters\n        params = sim.get_params()\n        self.EA = params[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = params[\"J\"]\n        self.R_roller = params[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = params[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = params[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n        # Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points\n        self.K_lqr_dict = {}\n        self._compute_adaptive_gains()\n\n    def _compute_steady_state(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_linearized_model(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute linearized state-space model at given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = self._compute_steady_state(T_ref)\n\n        # Operating point\n        x_op = np.concatenate([T_ref, v_ref])\n        u_op = self._compute_u_ref(x_op)\n\n        # Initialize matrices\n        A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n        B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = (1/self.L) * v_ref[i-1]\n            A[i, i] = -(1/self.L) * v_ref[i]\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[i, 6+i] = self.EA/self.L - T_ref[i]/self.L\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, 6+i-1] = self.EA/self.L + T_ref[i-1]/self.L\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            row = 6 + i\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i] = -self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n            A[row, i] = A[row, i] + self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_{i+1}\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i+1] = self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[row, 6+i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/du_i\n            B[row, i] = self.R_roller / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        for i in range(6):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < 5 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R_roller * x_ref[6+i] - self.R_roller * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self, A, B):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR gain for given A, B matrices.\"\"\"\n        n_states = 12\n        n_inputs = 6\n\n        # Discretize\n        I = np.eye(n_states)\n        A_d = I + self.dt * A\n        B_d = self.dt * B\n\n        # Solve discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation iteratively\n        P = self.Q.copy()\n        tolerance = 1e-10\n        max_iterations = 200\n\n        for _ in range(max_iterations):\n            P_new = self.Q + A_d.T @ P @ A_d - \\\n                    A_d.T @ P @ B_d @ np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n\n            if np.linalg.norm(P_new - P) < tolerance:\n                P = P_new\n                break\n            P = P_new\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        K = np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n        return K\n\n    def _compute_adaptive_gains(self):\n        \"\"\"Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point 1: Initial tensions\n        T_ref_1 = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        A_1, B_1 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_1)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['initial'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_1, B_1)\n\n        # Operating point 2: Final tensions\n        T_ref_2 = self.sim.T_ref_final\n        A_2, B_2 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_2)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['final'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_2, B_2)\n\n        # Current gain (will be interpolated)\n        self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].copy()\n\n    def _select_gain(self, x_current, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Select appropriate gain based on current and reference states.\"\"\"\n        # Interpolate between initial and final gains based on T3\n        T3_current = x_current[2]\n        T3_ref = x_ref[2]\n\n        # Use reference to determine which region we're in\n        if T3_ref < 30:  # Before step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial']\n        else:  # After step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['final']\n\n    def solve_mpc(self, x_current, x_ref_future):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state [12x1]\n            x_ref_future: Future reference states [12 x horizon_N]\n\n        Returns:\n            u_optimized: Optimal control input [6x1]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Select appropriate gain\n        x_ref0 = x_ref_future[:, 0]\n        self._select_gain(x_current, x_ref0)\n\n        # Use the linearized model for prediction\n        if x_ref0[2] < 30:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        else:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_final\n\n        A, B = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref)\n\n        # Discrete-time model\n        Ad = np.eye(12) + self.dt * A\n        Bd = self.dt * B\n\n        # Simple optimization: try different control scales\n        scales = np.linspace(0.8, 2.8, 15)\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n        u_best = np.zeros(6)\n\n        for scale in scales:\n            # Compute control input\n            error = x_current - x_ref0\n            u_candidate = -scale * self.K_lqr @ error\n\n            # Clip to reasonable range (carefully tuned to avoid overshoot)\n            u_candidate = np.clip(u_candidate, [-3.8]*6, [3.8]*6)\n\n            # Predict future states\n            x_pred = x_current.copy()\n            cost = 0.0\n\n            for i in range(self.horizon_N):\n                x_ref = x_ref_future[:, min(i, self.horizon_N-1)]\n\n                # Predict next state\n                x_pred = Ad @ x_pred + Bd @ u_candidate\n\n                # Compute cost\n                error_pred = x_pred - x_ref\n                # Heavily penalize tension errors\n                weighted_error = error_pred.copy()\n                weighted_error[:6] *= 2.0\n                cost += weighted_error.T @ self.Q @ weighted_error\n\n                # Control cost\n                cost += u_candidate.T @ self.R @ u_candidate\n\n            if cost < best_cost:\n                best_cost = cost\n                u_best = u_candidate.copy()\n\n        return u_best\n\n    def get_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Get controller parameters for JSON output.\"\"\"\n        # Use the initial gain for the parameters\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": self.horizon_N,\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[0].tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[1].tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main simulation loop.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Storage for logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial state: T={x[:6]}, v={x[6:]}\")\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    print(f\"Running simulation for {sim_time}s...\")\n    for step in range(n_steps):\n        # Solve MPC\n        u_optimal = controller.solve_mpc(x, x_ref_future)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u_optimal)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u_optimal.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Mean steady-state error\n    steady_errors = []\n    for i in range(steady_start, steady_end):\n        steady_errors.append(np.mean(np.abs(tensions[i] - references[i, :6])))\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(steady_errors)\n\n    # Find settling time for T3\n    tolerance = 0.05 * abs(step_refs[0] - T_ref_initial[2])\n    settling_mask = np.abs(step_tensions - step_refs) < tolerance\n\n    # Find when it settles and stays settled for a while\n    settling_time = 4.0  # Default\n    for i in range(len(settling_mask) - 50):\n        if np.all(settling_mask[i:i+50]):\n            settling_time = step_times[i]\n            break\n\n    # Max and min tensions across all sections\n    max_tension = np.max(tensions)\n    min_tension = np.min(tensions)\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(steady_state_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\ndef check_targets(metrics):\n    \"\"\"Check if all performance targets are met.\"\"\"\n    return (metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n            metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n            metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n            metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0)\n\n\ndef save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics):\n    \"\"\"Save all output files.\"\"\"\n    # Default Q and R if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n        if R_diag is None:\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n        # Get system parameters\n        params = sim.get_params()\n        self.EA = params[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = params[\"J\"]\n        self.R_roller = params[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = params[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = params[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n        # Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points\n        self.K_lqr_dict = {}\n        self._compute_adaptive_gains()\n\n    def _compute_steady_state(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_linearized_model(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute linearized state-space model at given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = self._compute_steady_state(T_ref)\n\n        # Operating point\n        x_op = np.concatenate([T_ref, v_ref])\n        u_op = self._compute_u_ref(x_op)\n\n        # Initialize matrices\n        A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n        B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = (1/self.L) * v_ref[i-1]\n            A[i, i] = -(1/self.L) * v_ref[i]\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[i, 6+i] = self.EA/self.L - T_ref[i]/self.L\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, 6+i-1] = self.EA/self.L + T_ref[i-1]/self.L\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            row = 6 + i\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i] = -self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n            A[row, i] = A[row, i] + self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_{i+1}\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i+1] = self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[row, 6+i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/du_i\n            B[row, i] = self.R_roller / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        for i in range(6):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < 5 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R_roller * x_ref[6+i] - self.R_roller * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self, A, B):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR gain for given A, B matrices.\"\"\"\n        n_states = 12\n        n_inputs = 6\n\n        # Discretize\n        I = np.eye(n_states)\n        A_d = I + self.dt * A\n        B_d = self.dt * B\n\n        # Solve discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation iteratively\n        P = self.Q.copy()\n        tolerance = 1e-10\n        max_iterations = 200\n\n        for _ in range(max_iterations):\n            P_new = self.Q + A_d.T @ P @ A_d - \\\n                    A_d.T @ P @ B_d @ np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n\n            if np.linalg.norm(P_new - P) < tolerance:\n                P = P_new\n                break\n            P = P_new\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        K = np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n        return K\n\n    def _compute_adaptive_gains(self):\n        \"\"\"Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point 1: Initial tensions\n        T_ref_1 = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        A_1, B_1 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_1)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['initial'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_1, B_1)\n\n        # Operating point 2: Final tensions\n        T_ref_2 = self.sim.T_ref_final\n        A_2, B_2 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_2)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['final'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_2, B_2)\n\n        # Current gain (will be interpolated)\n        self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].copy()\n\n    def _select_gain(self, x_current, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Select appropriate gain based on current and reference states.\"\"\"\n        # Interpolate between initial and final gains based on T3\n        T3_current = x_current[2]\n        T3_ref = x_ref[2]\n\n        # Use reference to determine which region we're in\n        if T3_ref < 30:  # Before step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial']\n        else:  # After step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['final']\n\n    def solve_mpc(self, x_current, x_ref_future):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state [12x1]\n            x_ref_future: Future reference states [12 x horizon_N]\n\n        Returns:\n            u_optimized: Optimal control input [6x1]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Select appropriate gain\n        x_ref0 = x_ref_future[:, 0]\n        self._select_gain(x_current, x_ref0)\n\n        # Use the linearized model for prediction\n        if x_ref0[2] < 30:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        else:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_final\n\n        A, B = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref)\n\n        # Discrete-time model\n        Ad = np.eye(12) + self.dt * A\n        Bd = self.dt * B\n\n        # Simple optimization: try different control scales\n        scales = np.linspace(0.8, 2.8, 15)\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n        u_best = np.zeros(6)\n\n        for scale in scales:\n            # Compute control input\n            error = x_current - x_ref0\n            u_candidate = -scale * self.K_lqr @ error\n\n            # Clip to reasonable range (carefully tuned to avoid overshoot)\n            u_candidate = np.clip(u_candidate, [-3.8]*6, [3.8]*6)\n\n            # Predict future states\n            x_pred = x_current.copy()\n            cost = 0.0\n\n            for i in range(self.horizon_N):\n                x_ref = x_ref_future[:, min(i, self.horizon_N-1)]\n\n                # Predict next state\n                x_pred = Ad @ x_pred + Bd @ u_candidate\n\n                # Compute cost\n                error_pred = x_pred - x_ref\n                # Heavily penalize tension errors\n                weighted_error = error_pred.copy()\n                weighted_error[:6] *= 2.0\n                cost += weighted_error.T @ self.Q @ weighted_error\n\n                # Control cost\n                cost += u_candidate.T @ self.R @ u_candidate\n\n            if cost < best_cost:\n                best_cost = cost\n                u_best = u_candidate.copy()\n\n        return u_best\n\n    def get_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Get controller parameters for JSON output.\"\"\"\n        # Use the initial gain for the parameters\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": self.horizon_N,\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[0].tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[1].tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main simulation loop.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Controller parameters (focused on tension tracking)\n    horizon_N = 11\n    Q_diag = [150.0, 150.0, 150.0, 150.0, 150.0, 150.0,  # High weight on tensions\n              10.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0]        # Low weight on velocities\n    R_diag = [0.05] * 6    # Moderate damping\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    print(\"Initializing MPC Controller with Adaptive Gains...\")\n    controller = R2R_MPCController(sim, horizon_N=horizon_N, Q_diag=Q_diag, R_diag=R_diag)\n\n    # Simulation parameters\n    dt = sim.dt\n    sim_time = 5.0\n    n_steps = int(sim_time / dt)\n\n    # Storage for logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial state: T={x[:6]}, v={x[6:]}\")\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    print(f\"Running simulation for {sim_time}s...\")\n    for step in range(n_steps):\n        # Get reference at current time\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(sim.t_idx)\n\n        # Get future references for MPC\n        x_ref_future = np.zeros((12, horizon_N))\n        for i in range(horizon_N):\n            future_idx = min(sim.t_idx + i, controller.sim.x_ref.shape[1] - 1)\n            x_ref_future[:, i] = controller.sim.x_ref[:, future_idx]\n\n        # Solve MPC\n        u_optimal = controller.solve_mpc(x, x_ref_future)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u_optimal)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u_optimal.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Update state\n        x = x_next\n\n        # Print progress\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    print(\"Simulation complete!\")\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    print(\"Computing performance metrics...\")\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim.T_ref_initial, sim.T_ref_final, sim.step_time)\n\n    # Save outputs\n    print(\"Saving outputs...\")\n    save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics)\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n=== Performance Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Mean steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(\"\\nAll targets achieved!\" if check_targets(metrics) else \"\\nSome targets missed.\")\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry[\"tensions\"] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry[\"time\"] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry[\"references\"] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Find step index\n    step_idx = int(step_time / 0.01)\n\n    # Steady-state error (after step, before end)\n    steady_start = min(step_idx + 100, len(tensions) - 100)  # 1s after step\n    steady_end = len(tensions)\n\n    # Mean steady-state error\n    steady_errors = []\n    for i in range(steady_start, steady_end):\n        steady_errors.append(np.mean(np.abs(tensions[i] - references[i, :6])))\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(steady_errors)\n\n    # Settling time (time to reach within 5% of reference after step)\n    step_tensions = tensions[step_idx:, 2]  # T3 is most relevant\n    step_times = times[step_idx:] - times[step_idx]\n    step_refs = references[step_idx:, 2]\n\n    # Find settling time for T3\n    tolerance = 0.05 * abs(step_refs[0] - T_ref_initial[2])\n    settling_mask = np.abs(step_tensions - step_refs) < tolerance\n\n    # Find when it settles and stays settled for a while\n    settling_time = 4.0  # Default\n    for i in range(len(settling_mask) - 50):\n        if np.all(settling_mask[i:i+50]):\n            settling_time = step_times[i]\n            break\n\n    # Max and min tensions across all sections\n    max_tension = np.max(tensions)\n    min_tension = np.min(tensions)\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(steady_state_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\ndef check_targets(metrics):\n    \"\"\"Check if all performance targets are met.\"\"\"\n    return (metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n            metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n            metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n            metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0)\n\n\ndef save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics):\n    \"\"\"Save all output files.\"\"\"\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = controller.get_controller_params()\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Outputs saved to:\")\n    print(\"  - controller_params.json\")\n    print(\"  - control_log.json\")\n    print(\"  - metrics.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":254,"oldLines":11,"newStart":254,"newLines":10,"lines":["     print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")","     sim = R2RSimulator()"," ","-    # Small penalty on control changes\n                u_prev_local = u_candidate.copy()","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMPC Controller for 6-section Roll-to-Roll manufacturing line (Version 2).\nUses adaptive gain scheduling for better performance.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\nclass R2R_MPCController:\n    \"\"\"MPC Controller with adaptive gain scheduling.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, sim, horizon_N=9, Q_diag=None, R_diag=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize MPC controller.\n\n        Args:\n            sim: R2RSimulator instance\n            horizon_N: Prediction horizon (3-30)\n            Q_diag: Diagonal of state cost matrix (12 elements)\n            R_diag: Diagonal of control cost matrix (6 elements)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.sim = sim\n        self.horizon_N = horizon_N\n        self.dt = sim.dt\n\n        # Default Q and R if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n        if R_diag is None:\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n        # Get system parameters\n        params = sim.get_params()\n        self.EA = params[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = params[\"J\"]\n        self.R_roller = params[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = params[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = params[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n        # Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points\n        self.K_lqr_dict = {}\n        self._compute_adaptive_gains()\n\n    def _compute_steady_state(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_linearized_model(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute linearized state-space model at given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = self._compute_steady_state(T_ref)\n\n        # Operating point\n        x_op = np.concatenate([T_ref, v_ref])\n        u_op = self._compute_u_ref(x_op)\n\n        # Initialize matrices\n        A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n        B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = (1/self.L) * v_ref[i-1]\n            A[i, i] = -(1/self.L) * v_ref[i]\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[i, 6+i] = self.EA/self.L - T_ref[i]/self.L\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, 6+i-1] = self.EA/self.L + T_ref[i-1]/self.L\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            row = 6 + i\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i] = -self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n            A[row, i] = A[row, i] + self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_{i+1}\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i+1] = self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[row, 6+i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/du_i\n            B[row, i] = self.R_roller / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        for i in range(6):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < 5 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R_roller * x_ref[6+i] - self.R_roller * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self, A, B):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR gain for given A, B matrices.\"\"\"\n        n_states = 12\n        n_inputs = 6\n\n        # Discretize\n        I = np.eye(n_states)\n        A_d = I + self.dt * A\n        B_d = self.dt * B\n\n        # Solve discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation iteratively\n        P = self.Q.copy()\n        tolerance = 1e-10\n        max_iterations = 200\n\n        for _ in range(max_iterations):\n            P_new = self.Q + A_d.T @ P @ A_d - \\\n                    A_d.T @ P @ B_d @ np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n\n            if np.linalg.norm(P_new - P) < tolerance:\n                P = P_new\n                break\n            P = P_new\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        K = np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n        return K\n\n    def _compute_adaptive_gains(self):\n        \"\"\"Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point 1: Initial tensions\n        T_ref_1 = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        A_1, B_1 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_1)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['initial'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_1, B_1)\n\n        # Operating point 2: Final tensions\n        T_ref_2 = self.sim.T_ref_final\n        A_2, B_2 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_2)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['final'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_2, B_2)\n\n        # Current gain (will be interpolated)\n        self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].copy()\n\n    def _select_gain(self, x_current, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Select appropriate gain based on current and reference states.\"\"\"\n        # Interpolate between initial and final gains based on T3\n        T3_current = x_current[2]\n        T3_ref = x_ref[2]\n\n        # Use reference to determine which region we're in\n        if T3_ref < 30:  # Before step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial']\n        else:  # After step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['final']\n\n    def solve_mpc(self, x_current, x_ref_future):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state [12x1]\n            x_ref_future: Future reference states [12 x horizon_N]\n\n        Returns:\n            u_optimized: Optimal control input [6x1]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Select appropriate gain\n        x_ref0 = x_ref_future[:, 0]\n        self._select_gain(x_current, x_ref0)\n\n        # Use the linearized model for prediction\n        if x_ref0[2] < 30:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        else:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_final\n\n        A, B = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref)\n\n        # Discrete-time model\n        Ad = np.eye(12) + self.dt * A\n        Bd = self.dt * B\n\n        # Simple optimization: try different control scales\n        scales = np.linspace(0.8, 2.8, 15)\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n        u_best = np.zeros(6)\n\n        for scale in scales:\n            # Compute control input\n            error = x_current - x_ref0\n            u_candidate = -scale * self.K_lqr @ error\n\n            # Clip to reasonable range (carefully tuned to avoid overshoot)\n            u_candidate = np.clip(u_candidate, [-3.8]*6, [3.8]*6)\n\n            # Predict future states\n            x_pred = x_current.copy()\n            cost = 0.0\n\n            for i in range(self.horizon_N):\n                x_ref = x_ref_future[:, min(i, self.horizon_N-1)]\n\n                # Predict next state\n                x_pred = Ad @ x_pred + Bd @ u_candidate\n\n                # Compute cost\n                error_pred = x_pred - x_ref\n                # Heavily penalize tension errors\n                weighted_error = error_pred.copy()\n                weighted_error[:6] *= 2.0\n                cost += weighted_error.T @ self.Q @ weighted_error\n\n                # Control cost\n                cost += u_candidate.T @ self.R @ u_candidate\n\n            if cost < best_cost:\n                best_cost = cost\n                u_best = u_candidate.copy()\n\n        return u_best\n\n    def get_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Get controller parameters for JSON output.\"\"\"\n        # Use the initial gain for the parameters\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": self.horizon_N,\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[0].tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[1].tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main simulation loop.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Controller parameters (best balance: horizon=12 gave T3=44.38N)\n    horizon_N = 12  # Best tracking from previous tests\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    print(\"Initializing MPC Controller with Adaptive Gains...\")\n    controller = R2R_MPCController(sim, horizon_N=horizon_N, Q_diag=Q_diag, R_diag=R_diag)\n\n    # Simulation parameters\n    dt = sim.dt\n    sim_time = 5.0\n    n_steps = int(sim_time / dt)\n\n    # Storage for logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial state: T={x[:6]}, v={x[6:]}\")\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    print(f\"Running simulation for {sim_time}s...\")\n    for step in range(n_steps):\n        # Get reference at current time\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(sim.t_idx)\n\n        # Get future references for MPC\n        x_ref_future = np.zeros((12, horizon_N))\n        for i in range(horizon_N):\n            future_idx = min(sim.t_idx + i, controller.sim.x_ref.shape[1] - 1)\n            x_ref_future[:, i] = controller.sim.x_ref[:, future_idx]\n\n        # Solve MPC\n        u_optimal = controller.solve_mpc(x, x_ref_future)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u_optimal)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u_optimal.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Update state\n        x = x_next\n\n        # Print progress\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    print(\"Simulation complete!\")\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    print(\"Computing performance metrics...\")\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim.T_ref_initial, sim.T_ref_final, sim.step_time)\n\n    # Save outputs\n    print(\"Saving outputs...\")\n    save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics)\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n=== Performance Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Mean steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(\"\\nAll targets achieved!\" if check_targets(metrics) else \"\\nSome targets missed.\")\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry[\"tensions\"] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry[\"time\"] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry[\"references\"] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Find step index\n    step_idx = int(step_time / 0.01)\n\n    # Steady-state error (after step, before end)\n    steady_start = min(step_idx + 100, len(tensions) - 100)  # 1s after step\n    steady_end = len(tensions)\n\n    # Mean steady-state error\n    steady_errors = []\n    for i in range(steady_start, steady_end):\n        steady_errors.append(np.mean(np.abs(tensions[i] - references[i, :6])))\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(steady_errors)\n\n    # Settling time (time to reach within 5% of reference after step)\n    step_tensions = tensions[step_idx:, 2]  # T3 is most relevant\n    step_times = times[step_idx:] - times[step_idx]\n    step_refs = references[step_idx:, 2]\n\n    # Find settling time for T3\n    tolerance = 0.05 * abs(step_refs[0] - T_ref_initial[2])\n    settling_mask = np.abs(step_tensions - step_refs) < tolerance\n\n    # Find when it settles and stays settled for a while\n    settling_time = 4.0  # Default\n    for i in range(len(settling_mask) - 50):\n        if np.all(settling_mask[i:i+50]):\n            settling_time = step_times[i]\n            break\n\n    # Max and min tensions across all sections\n    max_tension = np.max(tensions)\n    min_tension = np.min(tensions)\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(steady_state_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\ndef check_targets(metrics):\n    \"\"\"Check if all performance targets are met.\"\"\"\n    return (metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n            metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n            metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n            metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0)\n\n\ndef save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics):\n    \"\"\"Save all output files.\"\"\"\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = controller.get_controller_params()\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Outputs saved to:\")\n    print(\"  - controller_params.json\")\n    print(\"  - control_log.json\")\n    print(\"  - metrics.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":213,"oldLines":6,"newStart":213,"newLines":7,"lines":["             # Default Q and R if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n        if R_diag is None:\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n        # Get system parameters\n        params = sim.get_params()\n        self.EA = params[\"EA\"]\n        self.J = params[\"J\"]\n        self.R_roller = params[\"R\"]\n        self.fb = params[\"fb\"]\n        self.L = params[\"L\"]\n        self.v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n        # Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points\n        self.K_lqr_dict = {}\n        self._compute_adaptive_gains()\n\n    def _compute_steady_state(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _compute_linearized_model(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute linearized state-space model at given tension reference.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = self._compute_steady_state(T_ref)\n\n        # Operating point\n        x_op = np.concatenate([T_ref, v_ref])\n        u_op = self._compute_u_ref(x_op)\n\n        # Initialize matrices\n        A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n        B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = (1/self.L) * v_ref[i-1]\n            A[i, i] = -(1/self.L) * v_ref[i]\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[i, 6+i] = self.EA/self.L - T_ref[i]/self.L\n\n            # d(dT_i/dt)/dv_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, 6+i-1] = self.EA/self.L + T_ref[i-1]/self.L\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equations\n        for i in range(6):\n            row = 6 + i\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_i\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i] = -self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n            A[row, i] = A[row, i] + self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dT_{i+1}\n            if i < 5:\n                A[row, i+1] = self.R_roller**2 / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/dv_i\n            A[row, 6+i] = -self.fb / self.J\n\n            # d(dv_i/dt)/du_i\n            B[row, i] = self.R_roller / self.J\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _compute_u_ref(self, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state control inputs for given state.\"\"\"\n        u_ref = np.zeros(6)\n        for i in range(6):\n            T_next = x_ref[i+1] if i < 5 else 0\n            u_ref[i] = self.fb / self.R_roller * x_ref[6+i] - self.R_roller * (T_next - x_ref[i])\n        return u_ref\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self, A, B):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR gain for given A, B matrices.\"\"\"\n        n_states = 12\n        n_inputs = 6\n\n        # Discretize\n        I = np.eye(n_states)\n        A_d = I + self.dt * A\n        B_d = self.dt * B\n\n        # Solve discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation iteratively\n        P = self.Q.copy()\n        tolerance = 1e-10\n        max_iterations = 200\n\n        for _ in range(max_iterations):\n            P_new = self.Q + A_d.T @ P @ A_d - \\\n                    A_d.T @ P @ B_d @ np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n\n            if np.linalg.norm(P_new - P) < tolerance:\n                P = P_new\n                break\n            P = P_new\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        K = np.linalg.inv(self.R + B_d.T @ P @ B_d) @ B_d.T @ P @ A_d\n        return K\n\n    def _compute_adaptive_gains(self):\n        \"\"\"Pre-compute LQR gains for different operating points.\"\"\"\n        # Operating point 1: Initial tensions\n        T_ref_1 = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        A_1, B_1 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_1)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['initial'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_1, B_1)\n\n        # Operating point 2: Final tensions\n        T_ref_2 = self.sim.T_ref_final\n        A_2, B_2 = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref_2)\n        self.K_lqr_dict['final'] = self._compute_lqr_gain(A_2, B_2)\n\n        # Current gain (will be interpolated)\n        self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].copy()\n\n    def _select_gain(self, x_current, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"Select appropriate gain based on current and reference states.\"\"\"\n        # Interpolate between initial and final gains based on T3\n        T3_current = x_current[2]\n        T3_ref = x_ref[2]\n\n        # Use reference to determine which region we're in\n        if T3_ref < 30:  # Before step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['initial']\n        else:  # After step\n            self.K_lqr = self.K_lqr_dict['final']\n\n    def solve_mpc(self, x_current, x_ref_future):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state [12x1]\n            x_ref_future: Future reference states [12 x horizon_N]\n\n        Returns:\n            u_optimized: Optimal control input [6x1]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Select appropriate gain\n        x_ref0 = x_ref_future[:, 0]\n        self._select_gain(x_current, x_ref0)\n\n        # Use the linearized model for prediction\n        if x_ref0[2] < 30:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_initial\n        else:\n            T_ref = self.sim.T_ref_final\n\n        A, B = self._compute_linearized_model(T_ref)\n\n        # Discrete-time model\n        Ad = np.eye(12) + self.dt * A\n        Bd = self.dt * B\n\n        # Simple optimization: try different control scales\n        scales = np.linspace(0.8, 2.8, 15)\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n        u_best = np.zeros(6)\n\n        for scale in scales:\n            # Compute control input\n            error = x_current - x_ref0\n            u_candidate = -scale * self.K_lqr @ error\n\n            # Clip to reasonable range (carefully tuned to avoid overshoot)\n            u_candidate = np.clip(u_candidate, [-3.8]*6, [3.8]*6)\n\n            # Predict future states\n            x_pred = x_current.copy()\n            cost = 0.0\n            u_prev_local = np.zeros(6)  # Track control rate\n\n            for i in range(self.horizon_N):\n                x_ref = x_ref_future[:, min(i, self.horizon_N-1)]\n\n                # Predict next state\n                x_pred = Ad @ x_pred + Bd @ u_candidate\n\n                # Compute cost\n                error_pred = x_pred - x_ref\n                # Heavily penalize tension errors\n                weighted_error = error_pred.copy()\n                weighted_error[:6] *= 2.0\n                cost += weighted_error.T @ self.Q @ weighted_error\n\n                # Control cost\n                cost += u_candidate.T @ self.R @ u_candidate\n\n                # Control rate penalty (to reduce oscillations)\n                du = u_candidate - u_prev_local\n                cost += 0.01 * du.T @ du  # Small penalty on control changes\n                u_prev_local = u_candidate.copy()\n\n            if cost < best_cost:\n                best_cost = cost\n                u_best = u_candidate.copy()\n\n        return u_best\n\n    def get_controller_params(self):\n        \"\"\"Get controller parameters for JSON output.\"\"\"\n        # Use the initial gain for the parameters\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": self.horizon_N,\n            \"Q_diag\": np.diag(self.Q).tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": np.diag(self.R).tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr_dict['initial'].tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[0].tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self._compute_linearized_model(self.sim.T_ref_initial)[1].tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main simulation loop.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Initializing R2R Simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Controller parameters (best balance: horizon=12 gave T3=44.38N)\n    horizon_N = 12  # Best tracking from previous tests\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 12\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    print(\"Initializing MPC Controller with Adaptive Gains...\")\n    controller = R2R_MPCController(sim, horizon_N=horizon_N, Q_diag=Q_diag, R_diag=R_diag)\n\n    # Simulation parameters\n    dt = sim.dt\n    sim_time = 5.0\n    n_steps = int(sim_time / dt)\n\n    # Storage for logging\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial state: T={x[:6]}, v={x[6:]}\")\n\n    # Simulation loop\n    print(f\"Running simulation for {sim_time}s...\")\n    for step in range(n_steps):\n        # Get reference at current time\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(sim.t_idx)\n\n        # Get future references for MPC\n        x_ref_future = np.zeros((12, horizon_N))\n        for i in range(horizon_N):\n            future_idx = min(sim.t_idx + i, controller.sim.x_ref.shape[1] - 1)\n            x_ref_future[:, i] = controller.sim.x_ref[:, future_idx]\n\n        # Solve MPC\n        u_optimal = controller.solve_mpc(x, x_ref_future)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u_optimal)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u_optimal.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Update state\n        x = x_next\n\n        # Print progress\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={sim.get_time():.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n    print(\"Simulation complete!\")\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    print(\"Computing performance metrics...\")\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim.T_ref_initial, sim.T_ref_final, sim.step_time)\n\n    # Save outputs\n    print(\"Saving outputs...\")\n    save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics)\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n=== Performance Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"Mean steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(f\"Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.2f}N\")\n    print(\"\\nAll targets achieved!\" if check_targets(metrics) else \"\\nSome targets missed.\")\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry[\"tensions\"] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry[\"time\"] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry[\"references\"] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Find step index\n    step_idx = int(step_time / 0.01)\n\n    # Steady-state error (after step, before end)\n    steady_start = min(step_idx + 100, len(tensions) - 100)  # 1s after step\n    steady_end = len(tensions)\n\n    # Mean steady-state error\n    steady_errors = []\n    for i in range(steady_start, steady_end):\n        steady_errors.append(np.mean(np.abs(tensions[i] - references[i, :6])))\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(steady_errors)\n\n    # Settling time (time to reach within 5% of reference after step)\n    step_tensions = tensions[step_idx:, 2]  # T3 is most relevant\n    step_times = times[step_idx:] - times[step_idx]\n    step_refs = references[step_idx:, 2]\n\n    # Find settling time for T3\n    tolerance = 0.05 * abs(step_refs[0] - T_ref_initial[2])\n    settling_mask = np.abs(step_tensions - step_refs) < tolerance\n\n    # Find when it settles and stays settled for a while\n    settling_time = 4.0  # Default\n    for i in range(len(settling_mask) - 50):\n        if np.all(settling_mask[i:i+50]):\n            settling_time = step_times[i]\n            break\n\n    # Max and min tensions across all sections\n    max_tension = np.max(tensions)\n    min_tension = np.min(tensions)\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(steady_state_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\ndef check_targets(metrics):\n    \"\"\"Check if all performance targets are met.\"\"\"\n    return (metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n            metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n            metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n            metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0)\n\n\ndef save_outputs(controller, log_data, metrics):\n    \"\"\"Save all output files.\"\"\"\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = controller.get_controller_params()\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Outputs saved to:\")\n    print(\"  - controller_params.json\")\n    print(\"  - control_log.json\")\n    print(\"  - metrics.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":213,"oldLines":7,"newStart":213,"newLines":6,"lines":["             #
